# Mareld / Poseidon Nord - Project Resume
# =========================================
# Purpose: everything needed to continue working on the simulator
# AND the fgconfig.py GUI after a context reset. English only.

================================================================
0. LANGUAGE POLICY (HARD RULE)
================================================================
All code, comments, variable names, configuration files, UI strings,
print statements and documentation MUST be in English unless the user
explicitly requests otherwise. Swedish display names exist only as an
optional UI overlay (see sv_mapping in fgconfig.py).
User communication (chat answers, status updates): Swedish.

ASCII-ONLY RULE for this resume file (mareld_resume.txt):
Use plain ASCII characters only inside THIS file. Do NOT use
em-dash (U+2014), section sign (U+00A7), Swedish letters
(a-ring U+00E5, a-umlaut U+00E4, o-umlaut U+00F6) or other
non-ASCII glyphs HERE - they render as mojibake in some viewers.
Use "-" instead of em-dash, "Section N" instead of "SectionN"
with the section sign, and transliterate Swedish words (e.g.
"Paverkansdelta" instead of "Paverkansdelta" with a-ring).
Verify with:
    python3 -c "print(sum(1 for b in open('mareld_resume.txt','rb').read() if b>127))"
which must print 0.

IMPORTANT SCOPE: this ASCII-only restriction applies ONLY to
mareld_resume.txt. Swedish characters (a-ring, a-umlaut, o-umlaut,
etc.) MUST continue to work everywhere else - in user-facing chat
answers, in other documentation files, in GUI display strings
(e.g. sv_mapping in fgconfig.py), and in any text the user writes.
Do not strip or transliterate Swedish letters outside this file.

1. REPOSITORY LAYOUT (project root: /home/simon/eco-evo)
================================================================
  train.py                  Main ARS training entry point (requires --project).
                            Co-evolution default-on (--no_coevolution to opt
                            out). --run-name <name> isolates checkpoints in
                            results/<name>/. Per-generation snapshots: both
                            impact maps AND spawn biomass layouts are locked
                            for the whole generation via independent
                            gen_seed / gen_spawn_seed drawn in
                            _install_generation_maps (see section 27).
  inference.py              Inference runner for trained policies. Loads
                            results/<run-name>/policy_<fg>.pth (--run-name
                            mirrors train.py; --checkpoints overrides).
                            Installs frozen obs_mean/obs_var on env per-DM.
                            .npz impact-map loader is key-agnostic. See Section 17.
  run_mareld_mvp.py         Runs a full MVP simulation scenario.
  compare_scenarios.py      Compares "noll" vs "projekt" scenarios.
  mareld.yaml, mareld2.yaml Project manifest files (--project value).
  fgconfig/
    fgconfig.py             Tkinter GUI for editing FGs, impacts, matrices
                            AND per-FG spawn strategies (live preview).
                            See section 7.
    fg_library.yaml         Central biological library (species_definitions,
                            interaction_definitions, impact_definitions).
                            Holds library-default initial_biomass and
                            optional library-default `spawn:` block.
                            Per-FG: max_intake_rate (a), natural_mortality
                            (DM), seed_rate (NDM). Per-interaction:
                            assimilation_factor [0,1], handling_time (h),
                            preys_on, energy_gain (redundant, see Section
                            31). NOTE: max_intake_rate is no longer per
                            interaction (commit 9317a54).
    recent_projects.txt     GUI "Open Recent" persistence.
  lib/
    world/grid.py           Grid(width, height, cell_size, tick_duration).
    world/functional_group.py
                            FunctionalGroup: biomass, energy_reserve,
                            temp_energy_gains, is_decision_maker, hunger.
                            initialize_state: with randomize_energy=True,
                            s_X(0) ~ Uniform[u_X - 0.3, u_X + 0.3] clipped
                            to [0,1] per cell (alt. B - centred on per-FG
                            maintenance, break-even E[q_X(0)]=0). With
                            randomize_energy=False: R = B * initial_energy_ratio
                            * ME. See section 43.
                            min_split_biomass: indivisible-weight threshold
                            (kg in YAML/GUI; tonnes internally; 0 = continuous).
                            See section 20 for sub-threshold semantics.
    environments/ecosystem.py
                            EcosystemEnvironment: "The Tick" pipeline.
                            Heavily optimized (section 13). Constructor takes
                            ``observable_impact_vars``. _build_static_caches,
                            _batched_policy_forward via torch.bmm, float32
                            hot path. ARS-V2 obs normalisation.
    config/config_loader.py setup_full_mareld_mvp + load_project_config.
                            Both accept optional `seed` (CRN) AND optional
                            `spawn_seed` (per-generation biomass snapshot,
                            section 27). initial_biomass per FG: project
                            override > library default > 1000. Spawn
                            dispatches between (a) the new strategy-driven
                            path (lib/spawn/, section 26) when a `spawn:`
                            block exists, (b) legacy cluster-spawn with
                            per-cell floor 10*min_split_biomass otherwise.
                            New helpers: _build_spawn_spec(spawn_cfg,
                            default_seed) and _derive_fg_spawn_seed(base,
                            fg_id) (adler32-based per-FG sub-seed).
    runners/policy.py       Policy MLP.
    runners/trainer.py      ARS trainer; sequential + parallel paths.
                            CRN per delta-pair; alpha/beta fitness; ARS-V2
                            obs-norm (Welford merge) and top-b deltas;
                            persistent obs_stats[fg_id].
    runners/parallel_worker.py
                            multiprocessing worker (spawn ctx); ignores
                            SIGINT, throttles BLAS to 1 thread.
  tools/
    biomass_html.py         Offline interactive HTML generator (Plotly
                            via CDN). CLI: `python3 tools/biomass_html.py
                            <run_dir>` -> writes <run_dir>/plots.html.
                            Auto-regenerated by train.py after every
                            checkpoint (incl. Ctrl+C). See Section 42.
    starve_calibration.py   Stand-alone calibration tool: single-cell
                            rest-only starvation simulation per DM,
                            compares T1 (reserve depletion) and T_total
                            (95%% biomass loss) against literature
                            intervals embedded for Section 46 baselines.
                            CLI: `python3 tools/starve_calibration.py
                            --ticks 1500`. See Section 49.
  lib/spawn/                NEW spawn-strategy package (section 26).
    __init__.py             Public API: StrategySpec, make_weights,
                            distribute_with_floor.
    strategies.py           Four strategies: uniform, perlin, colony,
                            env_driven. Pure numpy, ~434 lines.
    allocator.py            distribute_with_floor: per-cell floor +
                            sum-preserving leftover redistribution.
  lib/viz/                  Live pygame visualiser package (Section 40).
                            Imported in main process only; never by workers.
    __init__.py             Re-exports LiveVisualizer.
    pygame_viz.py           LiveVisualizer class: per-FG biomass heatmaps
                            (viridis LUT, per-FG normalisation, optional
                            log1p) + colorbar strip + rolling reward/biomass
                            plot. Headless fallback (SDL_VIDEODRIVER=dummy)
                            and _NullViz no-op when pygame is not installed.
                            All public methods wrapped in try/except so
                            --visual never breaks train/inference.
  tests/                    42 tests, all green.
    test_allocator.py             14 tests (floor, sum, edges, determinism).
    test_spawn_integration.py     5 tests (legacy unchanged, YAML build,
                                  colony, uniform).
    test_uniform_bias_init.py     6 tests (bias init opt-in).
    test_phyto_zoo_sanity.py      5 tests (cc-bound, ratio bound,
                                  predation suppression, no NaN).
  make_*.py                 GIS helpers (60x60 PNG generators).
  *.png                     Geographic input layers (60x60 grayscale).
  results/<run-name>/       Per-run trained policies. Checkpoint format:
                            {'state_dict': ..., 'obs_stats': {...}}.
  Strategi.pdf, Mareld mini.pdf, MKB Mareld.pdf, Poseidon/
                            Source documents. Strategi.pdf is authoritative
                            for "The Tick".

================================================================
2. THE 8 FUNCTIONAL GROUPS (Mareld MVP)
================================================================
  Decision makers (have a policy network):
    zooplankton, pelagic_fish, gadoids, porpoises, seals, seabirds
  Non decision makers (logistic growth toward carrying capacity):
    phytoplankton, benthic_community

  English IDs are canonical. Swedish names only in FGConfigApp.sv_mapping.

================================================================
3. "THE TICK" PIPELINE (lib/environments/ecosystem.py)
================================================================
Random FG order each tick. Per tick - order per Method.pdf Section steg 1-6
(impact mortality applied BEFORE predation):
  _calculate_decisions -> _apply_impact_mortality -> _apply_predation
  -> _apply_movement -> _apply_growth

  1. _calculate_decisions  (batched over all DMs)
     - obs_batch (N_dm, max_in_dim, H, W). Each DM i writes a compact
       per-DM layout in the top per_dm_in_dim[i] slots; remaining slots
       are 0-padded. See Section 4 and Section 41 for the per-DM
       formula driven by the Observability matrix.
     - B_others channels for OTHER DMs use VISIBLE biomass
       B_visible = B_all * (1 - prev_hidden_frac), where
       prev_hidden_frac is the previous tick's pi_rest. ``Rest`` is a
       hide action (Section 41); the rested fraction is invisible to
       other FGs in the next tick. B_own (the observer itself) uses
       FULL biomass; an FG always sees its own full mass.
     - Raw-obs stats accumulated for trainer merge.
     - If env.obs_mean/obs_var set: obs clipped/normalised before forward.
     - All DM policies in one batched torch.bmm forward (weights padded
       to max_in_dim with zero columns for per-DM compact layout).
     - Output: pi_move (N_dm,4,H,W), pi_rest (N_dm,H,W), pi_eat
       (N_dm,N_all,H,W). Masked before renormalisation.
     - After pi_rest is finalised, prev_hidden_frac is rewritten to the
       current tick's pi_rest for use in the NEXT tick's observation.
  2. _apply_impact_mortality
     - Per (FG, impact): linear interpolation in impact_table; clip to
       endpoints. B reduced, R scaled so E=R/B preserved on survivors.
  3. _apply_predation  (fully vectorized)
     - hidden_frac_now = current tick's pi_rest (per DM-prey, NDMs=0).
       B_prey_visible = B_prey_all * (1 - hidden_frac_now). The hidden
       (rested) fraction is fully protected this tick.
     - D = B_pred*pi_eat*max_intake*hunger; with Holling-II,
       a_eff = a / (1 + a*h*B_prey_visible). Demand-cap uses
       B_prey_visible. Predator energy gain buffered to
       temp_energy_gains. See Section 41 for tick-asymmetry rationale.
  Sub-threshold cells: NO move-mask zeroing. Instead env replaces those
  cells' distribution with a one-hot argmax over the full 11-action set.
  See section 20.
  4. _apply_movement  (slice-assign over N_dm)
     - Per-action fraction pays metabolic cost * resting_metabolism.
     - Slice-assign (no np.roll). Energy follows biomass. temp_energy_gains
       added to stayers AFTER metabolism.
  5. _apply_growth
     - NDM: logistic dB = MG*B*(1 - B/CC); B clipped to [0, CC].
     - DM: s=R/(B*ME), u=maintenance_level (default 0.3), q=s-u,
           growth=B*MG*q.
         * Positive: only B updated (Variant B) -> energy dilution.
         * Negative: B and R scaled identically (E preserved).

================================================================
4. POLICY NETWORK DIMENSIONS
================================================================
Per cell the policy sees the von Neumann neighbourhood (the cell
itself + its 4 cardinal neighbours N, E, S, W) per Method.pdf. Out-
of-bounds neighbours are zero-padded. Neighbour order is fixed
N, E, S, W (matches AccessN/E/S/W in Method.pdf). Built by
lib/environments/ecosystem.py:_build_observation_batch and the
matching dimension calc is train.py:get_dynamic_policy_params.

Input layout (one observation vector per cell, per DM i):

  Let k_i = number of OTHER FGs that DM i observes per the Observability
  matrix (Section 41). Self is always implicitly observed via B_own and
  is NOT counted in k_i. Legacy projects without any _observes_ keys in
  interaction_definitions fall back to k_i = N_all - 1 (sees everyone),
  which is byte-identical to the pre-observability formula.

  center : [B_own, E_own, B_obs (k_i), impacts (n_obs)]
  N      : [B_own,        B_obs (k_i), impacts (n_obs)]
  E      : same shape as N
  S      : same shape as N
  W      : same shape as N

  center_dim_i = 1 + 1 + k_i + n_obs   # B_own + E_own + obs + impacts
  nbr_dim_i    = 1     + k_i + n_obs   # B_own + obs + impacts (no E)
  in_dim_i     = center_dim_i + 4 * nbr_dim_i

  max_in_dim   = max over DMs of in_dim_i (used for batched bmm padding)

Channel semantics:
  B_own      Own-FG biomass at that cell (tonnes; raw, before obs
             normalisation). Tells the policy "how many of me are
             here / next door".
  E_own      Own-FG energy fill ratio s_X = R / (B * ME) at the
             center cell, in [0, 1]. Only on the center (neighbour
             energy is intentionally NOT exposed - Method.pdf).
             Drives hunger: low s_X -> hungrier -> stronger Eat.
  B_obs      Biomass of every OTHER FG the DM is configured to
             observe (k_i channels), in stable global order
             (env.obs_others_idx[i] indexes into global_fg_order).
             Includes both DMs and NDMs that this DM observes;
             excludes self (covered by B_own) and any FG that this
             DM does NOT observe per the Observability matrix.
             Same k_i channels appear on center and on each of the
             4 neighbours so the policy can compare local vs
             one-step-away density. VALUES are visible biomass
             B_visible = B * (1 - prev_hidden_frac) (Section 41):
             the fraction of the observed FG that rested last tick
             is invisible.
  impacts    Values of every impact_id flagged "Observable by
             policy" in the project (one channel per id, e.g.
             windfarm_noise). Raw map values; muted/unobservable
             impacts are excluded from the input but still applied
             in _apply_impact_mortality.

Output layout (one logit vector per cell, per DM):

  out_dim = 5 + N_all_fgs
          = [Move_N, Move_E, Move_S, Move_W, Rest, Eat_<fg1>, ...
             Eat_<fgN>]

  The 4 Move slots correspond to the same N, E, S, W order as the
  neighbour observation channels. The Eat block has one slot per
  FG in global_fg_order; slots outside the predator's menu are
  permanently masked to 0 by the environment (so the network can
  emit them but they never fire). Final action probabilities come
  from softmax(logits / T), with T annealed from --temp_start to
  --temp_end over --temp_anneal_gens generations.

Backwards-compat note: an earlier resume revision listed
  in_dim = 2 + (N_fgs - 1) + n_observable_impacts
which is exactly center_dim above with k_i=N_all-1. That formula
was incomplete - it ignored the 4 * nbr_dim contribution from the
von Neumann neighbours. Current (and correct) formula is the
per-DM one above; setting k_i = N_all - 1 reproduces the legacy
"see everyone" behaviour bit-for-bit.

Batched bmm with per-DM in_dim_i: env._rebuild_batched_weights pads
each DM's W1 to (max_in_dim, hid) with zero rows for the unused
top slots. Since the observation tensor also pads those slots with
0, the padded rows contribute nothing to any DM's forward. Hidden
width and out_dim remain uniform across DMs (out_dim = 5 + N_all).

Checkpoint invalidation: changing the Observability matrix (k_i
shifts), toggling "Observable by policy" on impacts (changing
n_obs), adding/removing an FG (changing N_all), or changing the
grid in a way that affects per-cell layout changes in_dim_i and
invalidates existing .pth checkpoints (full retraining required).
The 4x neighbour expansion means even one new observable impact
bumps in_dim_i by 5, not 1.

5. CLI - current flag set for train.py
================================================================
Mandatory: --project <path-to.yaml>

Flag                  Default              Meaning
--visual              False                Live pygame visualiser (heatmaps
                                           + per-DM reward plot). See Sec 40.
--mortality           off                  on|off. Apply density-independent
                                           natural_mortality term in
                                           _apply_growth DM branch. Default
                                           OFF (Section 35.3 opt-in).
--migration           off                  on|off. When 'on', movement out
                                           through grid edges is not masked;
                                           out-of-grid mass is interpreted
                                           as emigration + immigration over
                                           edge cells (proportional to
                                           accessibility). Default OFF
                                           (Section 59 opt-in). Mirrored on
                                           inference.py.
--run-name            "default"            results/<run-name>/ for save/resume.
--coevolution         True                 Joint rollout co-evo. Use
                                           --no_coevolution for round-robin.
--profile             None                 sanity | info | deep. Explicit CLI
                                           flags override profile values.
                                           Profiles force action-hack-free
                                           settings (argmax_penalty=0,
                                           entropy_coef=0, temp=1.0/1.0,
                                           uniform_bias_init=False).
--species             ["all"]
--generations         "inf"
--iter-per-gen        20
--n_eval_ticks        2
--lr                  0.03
--sigma               0.1
--alpha               1.0
--beta                1.0
--n_deltas            10
--top_deltas          None -> n_deltas//2  ARS-V2 top-b.
--no_obs_normalize    False
--workers             0 (auto)
--grid                None (60x60)
--argmax_penalty      0.3 (CLI default)    Profiles force 0.0.
--entropy_coef        0.1 (CLI default)    Profiles force 0.0.
--temp_start/--temp_end/--temp_anneal_gens
                       3.0 / 1.0 / 10      Profiles force 1.0/1.0.
--uniform_bias_init   False
--policynetwork       [2, 30, sig]         LAYERS NODES [ACTIVATION]. Hidden
                                           layer count + width + activation
                                           ('sig'|'relu'|'tanh'). 3rd arg
                                           optional, defaults to 'sig'
                                           (legacy sigmoid). See Section 44.
--cappa               1.0                  Section 48 Variant B time-to-death
                                           bonus weight: fitness +=
                                           cappa*(t_survive/n_ticks). Default
                                           ON; set --cappa 0.0 for legacy
                                           byte-identical parity. See Sec 48.
--survival_threshold  0.01                 Fraction of b0 below which biomass
                                           is considered 'dead' for the
                                           t_survive tracking (default 1%%).
--resume              False
--rollouts_per_delta  1 (CLI default)      Number of independent worlds
                                           (impact + spawn snapshots) averaged
                                           per delta. Profiles sanity/info/deep
                                           force 3. M=1 is byte-identical to
                                           legacy single-world path. See
                                           Section 28.
--worlds_refresh      "iteration"          When to resample WorldList:
                                           "iteration" (new M worlds each ARS
                                           iter, recommended) or "generation"
                                           (lock M worlds for whole gen).
--rollouts_per_delta_schedule
                      None                 e.g. "1@0,3@10,5@50": M=1 gen 0-9,
                                           M=3 gen 10-49, M=5 from gen 50.
                                           Overrides --rollouts_per_delta.
--rnd_baseline        "none"               [all|solo|none], optional meta-arg.
                                           Bare --rnd_baseline == "all" for
                                           back-compat. 'all': single probe
                                           env where every DM acts uniformly
                                           at random simultaneously; all FGs
                                           (incl. NDMs) get an _rnd trace via
                                           trophic cascade. 'solo': leave-
                                           one-out - one probe env per DM-FG,
                                           only that FG is randomised, the
                                           others use trainer.policies; only
                                           DM-FGs get _rnd traces. 'none':
                                           disabled (default). Requires
                                           --visual to be visible. See
                                           Section 62.

Session header prints every flag with "(default)"/"(user)" tag, including
a "Rollouts/Delta" line that shows M (or the schedule) plus refresh policy.

Other entry points:
  python3 run_mareld_mvp.py
  python3 compare_scenarios.py
  python3 fgconfig/fgconfig.py

================================================================
6. PROJECT FILE FORMAT (mareld2.yaml)
================================================================
  project_metadata: {name: ..., reference_grid_width: 60,
                     reference_grid_height: 60}
  simulation_settings: {...}
  decision_makers:
    - group_id: zooplankton
      initial_biomass: 35000
      spawn:                        # OPTIONAL per-FG spawn override
        mode: perlin                # uniform|perlin|colony|env_driven
        scale: 12.0
        octaves: 4
        persistence: 0.5
        lacunarity: 2.0
        threshold: 0.0
        # seed: 42                  # optional; if omitted, derived per
                                    # generation from gen_spawn_seed
    - ...
  non_decision_makers:
    - {group_id: phytoplankton, initial_biomass: 80000}
    - {group_id: benthic_community, initial_biomass: 160000}
  impact_variables:
    - impact_id: windfarm_noise
      value_min: 0
      value_max: 140
      observable: true
      # muted: true                 # optional

initial_biomass lives in the project file (editable from GUI).
Resolution: project override > library default > 1000.
Spawn block resolution: project FG override > library
species_definitions.<id>.spawn > legacy uniform Dirichlet.

Per-impact value range (value_min/value_max) is per PROJECT and drives:
  - random sampling of impact maps during training/inference;
  - live range-validation in the Impact Table Editor's Value column;
  - help text in that editor.

================================================================
7. fgconfig.py - GUI STRUCTURE
================================================================
Tabs:
  "Project & FGs"       Scrollable both axes.
    Project Info: project name + Reference Grid (X x Y, min 3).
    fg_container with two side-by-side LabelFrames:
      - "Decision Makers"     (self.fg_listbox)
      - "Non Decision Makers" (self.ndm_listbox)
    Each: Add from Library / Add New FG / Remove FG.
    (Impact Variables list + Impact Editor were moved out to the new
    "Impacts" tab; see below.)
    TWO FG Editor frames, only one packed at a time:
      self.editor_frame  (DM): max_energy_reserve, energy_content,
        resting_metabolism, maintenance_level, growth_rate,
        starve_rate, visibility_floor (Section 51),
        natural_mortality, max_intake_rate, movement_speed, action
        costs (feeding/movement), "Indivisible Weight (kg)"
        (min_split_biomass), initial_biomass (ton).
      self.ndm_editor_frame (NDM): growth_rate,
        max_carrying_capacity, initial_biomass (ton).
      Both contain Apply Changes button followed by a NESTED
      "Spawn Strategy" LabelFrame (see below).

  Spawn Strategy (nested LabelFrame inside each FG Editor):
    - Mode dropdown showing capitalised display labels
      ("Uniform", "Perlin", "Colony", "Env-driven") backed by
      SPAWN_MODE_LABELS / SPAWN_MODE_KEYS; internal YAML keys remain
      lowercase ("uniform", "perlin", "colony", "env_driven").
    - Dynamic parameter pane (per-mode via SPAWN_PARAM_SCHEMA).
      Parameter names in the Info panel are capitalised (Scale,
      Octaves, Persistence, Lacunarity, Threshold, N_colonies,
      Sigma_cells, Anchor, Floor, Noise_amp, Noise_scale).
    - 120x120 live preview canvas, debounced re-render via
      lib.spawn.make_weights -> Tk PhotoImage. Reacts to mode and
      param changes.
    - Info panel (English help text per mode) from SPAWN_MODE_HELP.
    - apply_fg_changes writes the spawn block back to:
        * DM:  the project FG entry's `spawn:` key
        * NDM: the library species_definitions entry (via the same
          _collect_spawn_dict helper).
    Implementation helpers: _build_spawn_editor, _rebuild_spawn_params,
    _collect_spawn_dict, _populate_spawn_editor, _update_spawn_help.

  "Impacts"             Dedicated tab (added after "Project & FGs").
                        Contains:
    - "Impact Variables in Project" listbox (self.impact_listbox)
      with Add from Library / Remove Impact / Mute buttons.
    - Impact Editor (self.impact_editor_frame, was previously inside
      Project & FGs).

  Impact Editor (inside the "Impacts" tab):
    - Min/Max value entries for the selected impact.
    - "Observable by policy" checkbox; toggling shows messagebox
      stating retraining is required.
    - Disabled when the selected impact is muted.

  Impact Table Editor (per (fg, impact) cell):
    - Columns: value, biomass_factor, energy_factor.
    - value clamped to [value_min, value_max] when set.
    - biomass_factor / energy_factor clamped to [0, 1].

  "FG Interactions"     Tab containing per-(predator,prey) matrices:
                        Predation (bool, row eats column),
                        Observability (bool, row observes column -
                        see Section 41),
                        Assimilation Factor (float [0,1]),
                        Handling time (nonneg float).
                        Non-decision-makers appear as COLUMNS only
                        (potential prey / observable targets); they
                        never appear as rows (no policy network).
                        Self-references in Observability are forced
                        True and disabled (a DM always sees itself).
                        Predation cells are linked to Observability:
                        x preys_on y forces x observes y = True and
                        disables that observability cell.
                        Muted FGs grey out both row- and column-cells
                        in all matrices, consistent with predation.

  "Impact Interactions" Impact Affects (bool, row impacted by column;
                        NDMs as columns only) + per-cell Impact Table
                        Editor button (value/biomass_factor/
                        energy_factor).

Key behaviours:
  - Add from Library filters by category; already-present FGs/impacts
    hidden in dialogs.
  - on_fg_select packs the matching editor; the nested spawn frame is
    a child of that editor and follows automatically.
  - _set_widget_tree_state recursively disables editor + spawn frame
    when the FG is muted.
  - Library writes via ruamel.yaml. Recent projects in
    recent_projects.txt.

================================================================
7b. REFERENCE GRID & BIOMASS SCALING (project_metadata)
================================================================
Each project stores a reference grid (Rx, Ry) in
project_metadata.reference_grid_width / reference_grid_height. All
biomass values entered in fgconfig (FG editor's initial_biomass_min/
max and the Inference tab's inference_initial_biomass) are defined
for that reference grid.

At runtime in load_project_config:

    biomass_scale = (H_act * W_act) / (Rx * Ry)

* mode='train':  min_b *= scale; max_b *= scale; min_b clamped to
                 >= 1.0 ton; max_b clamped to >= min_b.
* mode='inference': total_b = max(inference_initial_biomass * scale, 1.0).

Legacy projects without reference_grid_* keys fall back to 60x60.
Sub-3 values are rounded up to 3 by _as_pos_int.

================================================================
8. fg_library.yaml - WHAT LIVES WHERE
================================================================
  species_definitions:
    <fg_id>:
      display_name, is_decision_maker
      initial_biomass                      (library-default fallback only)
      growth_rate, max_carrying_capacity   (NDM only)
      seed_rate                            (NDM only; fraction of cc/tick)
      max_energy_reserve, energy_content,
      resting_metabolism, movement_speed,
      maintenance_level                    (u_X, default 0.3)
      feeding_cost, movement_cost          (resting_cost is hardcoded to
                                            1.0; stripped from YAML)
      max_intake_rate                      (DM only; a, physiological
                                            ceiling, ton prey / ton pred /
                                            tick; moved from interaction in
                                            commit 9317a54)
      natural_mortality                    (DM only; fraction/tick;
                                            density-independent, see
                                            --mortality flag, default OFF)
      min_split_biomass                    (DM only; kg, 0 = continuous)
      menu: [prey_id, ...]                 (DM only)
      spawn:                               # OPTIONAL library default
        mode: uniform|perlin|colony|env_driven
        # mode-specific params (Scale, Octaves, N_colonies, Sigma_cells,
        # Floor, Noise_amp, Noise_scale, ...)
        # seed: <int>                      # optional pin
  interaction_definitions:
    "<observer>_observes_<target>": {observes: bool}
        # Per-(observer, target) bit driving the policy input space.
        # Section 41. Self-pairs are implicitly True and not
        # editable in the GUI. Muted observer or target is excluded
        # entirely (muting == FG does not exist) - same semantics
        # as predation. If NO _observes_ keys exist for an observer
        # at all, legacy fallback applies (sees every other FG).
    "<predator>_preys_on_<prey>":  {preys_on, energy_gain,
                                    assimilation_factor (0..1, default 1.0),
                                    handling_time (h; 0 = pure Type I)}
        # max_intake_rate has been MOVED to species_definitions.<predator>
        # (commit 9317a54). Effective intake per (predator,prey) is:
        #   intake = a_pred * pi_eat * hunger * B_pred,
        # with energy gain = energy_gain * assimilation_factor.
        # When handling_time>0 anywhere, Type II saturation kicks in
        # globally (see Section 35.2).
    "<fg>_impacted_by_<impact>":   {impact_affects: bool,
                                    impact_table: [{value, biomass_factor,
                                                    energy_factor}, ...]}
        # impact_table: sorted by value; linear interpolation, clip to
        # endpoints. Old `impact_sensitivity` scalar removed everywhere.
  impact_definitions:  bottom_trawling, pelagic_trawling, hunting, logging,
                       chemicals, windfarm_noise, ship_traffic, turbidity.

================================================================
9. ARS TRAINER (lib/runners/trainer.py)
================================================================
ARSTrainer(env_builder, policy_params,
           sigma=0.1, lr=0.02, n_deltas=8,
           n_workers=1, alpha=1.0, beta=1.0,
           obs_normalize=True, top_deltas=None,
           entropy_coef=0.0, argmax_penalty=0.0,
           integral_reward=True, uniform_bias_init=False,
           hidden_layers=2, hidden_dim=30, activation="sig",
           survival_bonus=0.0, survival_threshold=0.01)

  survival_bonus / survival_threshold: Section 48 Variant B time-to-
  death bonus. CLI --cappa maps to survival_bonus (default 1.0 in
  train.py; the constructor itself still defaults to 0.0 for legacy
  parity in library users). Adds cappa*(t_survive/n_ticks) to per-FG
  fitness, where t_survive is first tick where biomass drops below
  survival_threshold*b0 (else n_ticks). Plumbed through both
  _evaluate, _evaluate_coevo and the parallel-worker mirrors.

  policies: persistent {fg_id: PolicyNetwork} across generations.
  obs_stats: persistent {fg_id: {mean (D,), var (D,), count}} across
             generations; frozen during a rollout, updated via Welford
             parallel merge after each train_step.
  top_deltas: None -> all n_deltas used.
  _pool: spawn-context Pool when n_workers > 1.

train_step(fg_to_train, n_eval_ticks=2):
  1. Sample n_deltas directions.
  2. Draw pair_seeds[i] (one per delta pair).
  3. Build (N_dm, D) obs_mean/obs_var from obs_stats.
  4. Evaluate +sigma*delta and -sigma*delta with SAME pair_seed (CRN).
  5. Parallel tasks: (fg, weights_snapshot, n_eval_ticks, alpha, beta,
     seed, obs_pack). Return (fitness, samples).
  6. Fitness: delta_b = log((bh+eps)/(b0+eps)), delta_r similar,
              fitness = alpha*delta_b + beta*delta_r
                        + survival_bonus*(t_survive/n_ticks)
              (last term off when survival_bonus=0). See Section 48.
  7. Welford parallel merge of obs_stats.
  8. ARS-V2 update with top-b sigma normalisation.

================================================================
10. CO-EVOLUTION LOOP (train.py main)
================================================================
Round-robin per species OR joint-rollout coevolution (default).
For each gen:
  - _refresh_world_list_if_needed(gen, iter) (re)samples WorldList =
    [(impact_seed_m, spawn_seed_m), ...] of length M, governed by
    --worlds_refresh (iteration|generation). Published to
    trainer.world_list and exposed as _current_world_list[0] for the
    legacy single-world install path.
  - _install_generation_worlds(gen) consumes _current_world_list[0]
    and builds _EnvBuilder with:
      * impact_seed -> impact map snapshot (impact_maps_snapshot)
      * spawn_seed  -> per-generation spawn snapshot, forwarded into
        load_project_config / setup_full_mareld_mvp so all
        deltas/workers in this generation see identical biomass maps
        per FG.
    _install_generation_maps is kept as a backward-compatible alias.
  - Worker pool rebuilt only when the WorldList actually (re)sampled.
  - For species in target_species: iter_per_gen train_step calls.
    Trainer's M-loop reads trainer.world_list and runs M rollouts per
    (delta, sign) pair with CRN coupling (see Section 28).
  - Generation header prints elapsed wall time, e.g.
    "========== Generation 2/100 (T=1.000) (01:14:55) ==========".
    Elapsed clock starts just before the first generation (after the
    interactive prompt), formatted as HH:MM:SS.
  - _save_checkpoint writes {'state_dict','obs_stats'} per generation.

KeyboardInterrupt handling: train.py calls os.setpgrp() at startup so
the parent process gets its own process group. TTY-delivered SIGINT
then only hits the parent (never the spawn-bootstrapping workers,
which would otherwise each dump a multi-page traceback during their
`import torch`). Workers also install signal.SIG_IGN in _worker_init
as backup. The parent catches KeyboardInterrupt, calls trainer.close()
(terminates pool fast), saves final checkpoints, returns cleanly.

================================================================
11. KNOWN DISCREPANCIES vs Strategi.pdf / Method.pdf (still open)
================================================================
  1. Observable impacts are opt-in per project (one obs channel each).
     Per-FG observability of OTHER FGs is also opt-in via the
     Observability matrix (Section 41); legacy projects without
     _observes_ keys default to "sees everyone".
  2. Memory term N(c, t-1) not implemented.
  3. Movement speed v > 1 not supported (cap 1.0).
  4. Positive "Paverkansdelta" (stocking) not supported.
  5. accessibility map: wired to movement masking AND (when --migration
     on, Section 59) to the edge-cell immigration weights. It IS plumbed
     through to spawn via distribute_with_floor(allowed_mask=...) and
     make_weights(context={'allowed_mask': ...}), but the caller in
     config_loader currently passes None until the project ships an
     accessibility/depth map.
  6. No persistent M*-layers; ARS scaling wins over Method.pdf.
  7. No runtime slider 0-5 + back-scaling pipeline yet.

================================================================
12. GRID / MAP CONVENTIONS
================================================================
Default 60x60, 1 km/cell, 6 h/tick. PNG maps mode "L".
SWEREF 99 TM bbox: E 211000..271000, N 6414000..6474000.
Row 0 = northernmost, column 0 = westernmost.

Initial spatial layout per FG:
  - If a `spawn:` block is present (project override > library
    default): lib.spawn.make_weights(StrategySpec, (H,W), context, seed)
    -> (H,W) weights -> distribute_with_floor(weights, total_b,
    min_per_cell=10*min_split_biomass, allowed_mask=None) -> initial
    biomass map.
  - Otherwise: legacy cluster-spawn (uniform Dirichlet + per-cell floor).
Same total biomass per FG within a rollout; layout deterministic from
spawn_seed when train.py supplies one (per-generation snapshot,
Section 27). With M>1 (Section 28), each of the M worlds in the
WorldList has its own (impact_seed_m, spawn_seed_m) pair, so M
distinct biomass maps are evaluated per delta-pair.

================================================================
13. PERFORMANCE NOTES (don't redo without measuring)
================================================================
Implemented:
  - Batched policy inference (torch.bmm).
  - Vectorized predation over (N_dm, N_all, H, W) tensors.
  - Slice-assign movement (no np.roll).
  - Preallocated/cached masks. float32 hot path.
  - Parallel rollouts via multiprocessing.Pool (spawn).
  - OMP/MKL/OPENBLAS/torch threads = 1 in workers.
  - CRN: same world for +delta and -delta in each ARS pair.
  - ARS-V2 obs normalisation + top-b deltas.
  - lib/spawn/ strategies are pure-numpy vectorised (no Numba/GPU
    needed at MVP grid sizes).

Intentionally NOT done:
  - ARS reward baseline subtraction (no-op for diff update).
  - Numba / GPU at current grid sizes.
  - Synchronous Nash-Q-like co-evolution.

================================================================
16. CHECKPOINT FORMAT COMPATIBILITY
================================================================
New format (saved by train.py):
    torch.save({'state_dict': policy.state_dict(),
                'obs_stats': {'mean': fp32(D,), 'var': fp32(D,),
                              'count': int}}, path)

Legacy format (older runs, pre-ARS-V2):
    torch.save(policy.state_dict(), path)

Recommended loader pattern:
    ckpt = torch.load(path, weights_only=False)
    if isinstance(ckpt, dict) and 'state_dict' in ckpt:
        policy.load_state_dict(ckpt['state_dict'])
        os = ckpt.get('obs_stats')
        if os is not None and apply_obs_norm:
            env.obs_mean = np.broadcast_to(os['mean'], (N_dm,D)).astype(
                np.float32).copy()
            env.obs_var  = np.broadcast_to(os['var'],  (N_dm,D)).astype(
                np.float32).copy()
    else:
        policy.load_state_dict(ckpt)         # legacy file

Notes:
- obs_stats: each .pth stores only its own species' stats.
- During inference, keep obs_stats frozen.
- Warm-up: first iteration sees obs_mean=0, obs_var=1.

================================================================
17. INFERENCE RUNNER (inference.py)
================================================================
Flags:
  --project <path>     Project YAML (default: built-in MVP).
  --run-name <name>    Loads from results/<name>/ (default "default").
  --checkpoints <dir>  Overrides --run-name.
  --grid n*m           Grid dims (default 60*60).
  --ticks N            (default 100)
  --seed N             Deterministic initial biomass.
  --output PATH.npz    Save per-FG biomass history.
  --quiet

Impact-map .npz loader (_load_impact_map_npz) is key-agnostic: matches
by id, otherwise picks first 2-D array, otherwise zero field.

Key behaviours:
  - Supports both new + legacy ckpt formats.
  - obs_mean/obs_var per-DM lookup (not positional) -> reorder-safe.
  - mean=0/var=1 fallback with loud warning for missing/empty stats.
  - Calls _build_static_caches() + _rebuild_batched_weights().
  - Does NOT update obs_stats during inference.
  - Inference path uses the SAME spawn dispatch as training
    (mode='inference', spawn_seed=None unless --seed sets one).

================================================================
18. RUN-NAME / CHECKPOINT ISOLATION
================================================================
Both train.py and inference.py accept --run-name (default "default").
  - train.py saves per-generation ckpts to results/<run-name>/
    policy_<fg>.pth (overwrites).
  - inference.py loads from results/<run-name>/ unless --checkpoints
    is given.
  - Old loose checkpoints at results/policy_*.pth are NOT auto-migrated.

================================================================
19. ACTION-HACK FLAGS + PROFILES
================================================================
Per konvergensproblem.txt's "INSIKT 2": action-shaping hacks are
symptom-painting; profiles disable them all.

Flags (all no-ops when zero/off):
  --argmax_penalty F        Profiles force 0.0.
  --entropy_coef F          Profiles force 0.0.
  --temp_start/--temp_end/--temp_anneal_gens
                            Profiles force T0=T1=1.0.
  --uniform_bias_init       Profiles keep off.
                            Tests: tests/test_uniform_bias_init.py (16/16).

Profiles (--profile sanity|info|deep):
  sanity  generations=10, iter_per_gen=15, n_deltas=16,
          n_eval_ticks=100, temp_anneal_gens=8
  info    generations=40, iter_per_gen=20, n_deltas=16,
          n_eval_ticks=150, temp_anneal_gens=30
  deep    generations=80, iter_per_gen=20, n_deltas=20,
          n_eval_ticks=200, temp_anneal_gens=60
All three set coevolution=True and force action-hack-free settings.

================================================================
20. SPAWN / SUB-THRESHOLD FIX (structural)
================================================================
Discovered cause of seals/porpoises "rest=100%" attractor:
min_split_biomass=50 kg combined with seals total=25 ton / 60x60 grid
= 6.94 kg/cell meant most cells were under-threshold; the old rule
zeroed move-actions, forcing rest=100%.

Two coordinated fixes:

DEL 1 - Cluster-spawn helper _spawn_biomass_distribution (legacy):
  * Eligible cells = allowed_mask>0 (None => full grid).
  * Per-cell floor = 10 * min_split_biomass.
  * Loop: random cell pick, biomass drawn uniformly in
    [floor, min(total_b, remaining)], assign, repeat.
  * Leftover (<floor) merged into a randomly chosen seeded cell.
  * If floor==0 -> legacy uniform Dirichlet.

DEL 1b - Strategy-driven spawn (lib/spawn/):
  When a `spawn:` block exists in project (per-FG) or library
  (per-species default), the helper goes through the new path:
    weights = make_weights(spec, (H,W), context, seed)
    initial_b = distribute_with_floor(weights, total_b,
                                      min_per_cell, allowed_mask=None)
  The allocator's floor semantics are unchanged so existing
  guarantees (no cell under floor; total preserved) still hold.
  See section 26 for the strategies.

DEL 2 - Sub-threshold collapse in ecosystem.py:
  * Removed move-mask zeroing on B < min_split.
  * After softmax, sub_thr_mask = (B>0) & (B<min_split) cells get a
    one-hot argmax over the full 11-action distribution. Whole group
    performs one action (including move) - small groups can migrate
    as a unit ("variant 2").

================================================================
21. CO-EVOLUTION (default training mode)
================================================================
Implemented in trainer.train_step_coevolution and worker
_evaluate_coevo_task. Default ON.

Mechanics:
  * Per ARS iteration: one independent delta per species; CRN
    rollout seed across the +/- halves.
  * 2*n_deltas joint rollouts with ALL DM policies perturbed
    simultaneously. Each rollout returns one fitness per species.
  * ARS update is per-species, using THAT species' own deltas and
    its own r_pos/r_neg vector. No cross-species delta leakage.
  * Predator/prey feedback materialises in the same rollout instead
    of being averaged out against frozen opponents.

Empirical signature (reproducible):
  * Zooplankton "escape" from rest=100% to ~rs/et=40/60 around gen
    13-20 once pelagic_fish predation pressure ramps up.
  * Seals/porpoises rest=100% attractor: structural, robust to every
    intervention tried.
  * Gadoids reach POSITIVE reward once hacks are off.
  * Pelagic_fish converges to mv-dominant (74-98%) "flee" policy.

Kept eval snapshots: results/coev_5gen_100ticks_eval,
results/coev_20gen_100ticks_eval, results/coev_info_profile_40gen,
results/coev_20gen_noHacks, results/coev_info_20gen_postSpawnFix.

================================================================
22. DESIGN PRINCIPLE: BIOLOGY DRIVES, NOT ACTION-HACKS
================================================================
Rule of thumb when adding a fix:
  1. If it shapes the action distribution (penalty/bonus on action
     fractions, entropy, temperature, eps-greedy, action bias init) ->
     ACTION-HACK. Hide behind a flag and keep OFF in profiles.
  2. If it changes the biological/structural setup (spawn
     distribution, min_split semantics, food web edges in
     fg_library.yaml, impact mortality tables, integral-reward,
     co-evolution rollout structure) -> STRUCTURAL fix. Prefer these.

The "evaluate by action histogram" lens is officially deprecated.
Quality must be evaluated by biomass trajectories over long inference
rollouts, predator-prey cycles, response to impact fields, and
comparison against Baltic-Sea field data.

Known open structural items:
  * benthic_community grows 27x over 10000-tick inference because no
    DM predates it and its K is soft.
  * No predator above seals/porpoises in mareld2.yaml -> top-predator
    rest-attractor is mechanistically rational.
  * accessibility map: plumbed into spawn (allowed_mask param +
    make_weights context) but currently passed as None at the caller.
  * min_split_biomass=50 kg still set for porpoises and seals.
  * Dead parameter: seals_preys_on_pelagic_fish has preys_on:false but
    leftover max_intake_rate: 0.012.

LATE-SESSION ADDITIONS (see konvergensproblem.txt for full story):
  * KRITISK INSIKT 7: 150-tick rollouts HIDE ecosystem collapse.
    2000-tick inference showed deterministic zooplankton extinction
    ~tick 620-700 even when training reward was positive.
  * Bugfix: _EnvBuilder now bakes in PROJECT_PATH (workers re-import
    train.py under spawn ctx and would otherwise fall back to MVP
    defaults).

================================================================
23. BIOLOGICAL SANITY CHECK FORMULA
================================================================
Per Strategi.pdf + konvergensproblem.txt "Kritisk insikt 4". For each
DM, compute per-tick energy balance at full hunger (h=1) and require
netto_eat > rest_cost.

SUPERSEDED BY SECTION 67: evaluating the intake side at h=1 is
invalid, because h=1 requires s_X=0 (an empty reserve - a state the
animal does not survive in). The gate must be evaluated at the
MAINTENANCE LEVEL u_X, where h(u_X) = 1 - u_X/0.8. The h=1 form
below is kept as the legacy back-compat gate only; the binding
condition is now netto_eat(u_X) > 0. See Section 67.

IMPORTANT (commit 9317a54): `max_intake_rate` (a) is now on the
species_definitions level (one per predator FG), not per interaction.
Assimilation is captured via per-interaction `assimilation_factor`
[0,1]. The energy intake per (pred, prey) is:

  intake_at_h1 = a_pred * energy_gain_pred_prey *
                 assimilation_factor_pred_prey       # best prey
  feed_cost    = feeding_cost    * resting_metabolism
  rest_cost    = resting_cost    * resting_metabolism  # resting_cost=1
  move_cost    = movement_cost   * resting_metabolism

  netto_eat = intake_at_h1 - feed_cost                 # MUST be > 0
  Hard gate:        netto_eat > rest_cost
  Soft gate (move): netto_eat > move_cost

The numbers below are pre-9317a54 baselines and need recomputation
against the new YAML; the inequalities themselves are unchanged.

Post-update (current YAML):
  zooplankton  netto_eat=+101.4  eat-rest=+98.9   eat-move=+101.4
  pelagic_fish netto_eat=+84.8   eat-rest=+76.8   eat-move=+67.6
  gadoids      netto_eat=+564.5  eat-rest=+558.5  eat-move=+553.4
  porpoises    netto_eat=+95.0   eat-rest=+33.0   eat-move=-91.0 *
  seals        netto_eat=+147.5  eat-rest=+122.5  eat-move=+85.0
  seabirds     netto_eat=+493.5  eat-rest=+403.5  eat-move=+133.5
All DMs pass the hard gate. Porpoises fails the soft gate (move > eat).

================================================================
24. SANITY-CHECK SNIPPET
================================================================
SCOPE: This snippet checks the ENERGY-BALANCE gates only, in the
absence of predation pressure. It is a necessary but NOT sufficient
condition for the YAML to be well-tuned. For DMs that are prey to
another DM and have visibility_floor < 1, the effective marginal
comparison eat-vs-rest ALSO includes a predation-avoidance term
(see 24.B below); rest may then be marginally preferred even when
eat-rest > 0 in pure energy terms. Do NOT use this snippet as an
isolated pass/fail verdict on rest-heavy policies for such DMs
(e.g. zooplankton with floor=0.2 under pelagic_fish predation, as
observed in run pzpg248nrnac).

24.A ENERGY-BALANCE GATES (no predation)

  rows = [
    # (name, I, E_prey, feeding_cost, SMR, resting_cost, movement_cost)
    ('zooplankton',  0.175, 600,  1.45, 2.5, 1.0, 0.0 ),
    ('pelagic_fish', 0.04,  2500, 1.9,  8.0, 1.0, 2.15),
    ('gadoids',      0.115, 5000, 1.75, 6.0, 1.0, 1.85),
    ('porpoises',    0.05,  5000, 2.5,  62., 1.0, 3.0 ),
    ('seals',        0.05,  4000, 2.1,  25., 1.0, 2.5 ),
    ('seabirds',     0.15,  5000, 2.85, 90., 1.0, 4.0 ),
  ]
  for n,I,E,fc,smr,rc,mc in rows:
      intake = I*E; feed = fc*smr; netto = intake - feed
      rest = rc*smr; move = mc*smr
      print(f"{n:14} netto={netto:8.1f} eat-rest={netto-rest:8.1f} "
            f"eat-move={netto-move:8.1f}")

Acceptance (hard gate)  : netto > 0             for every DM.
Acceptance (soft gate)  : eat-rest > 0          for every DM.
Note: porpoises may fail eat-move; that is a known accepted
outlier (see Section 23).

OBSOLETE - DO NOT USE THIS SNIPPET AS THE GATE. It evaluates the
intake at h=1 and therefore passes configurations that starve at
their own maintenance level (porpoises: netto=+166.5 at h=1 but
-16.3 at u_X). Replaced by lib/world/energy_balance.py +
tests/test_energy_balance_gate.py; run pytest instead of pasting
this snippet. See Section 67. The 24.B correction below is
unaffected and still applies on top of the u_X-based gate.

24.B PREDATION-AVOIDANCE CORRECTION (visibility_floor < 1)

For a DM X that is prey to predator Y and has visibility_floor
= floor_X < 1, the fraction of X visible to Y at pi_rest is:

  visible_frac(X) = 1 - pi_rest_X * (1 - floor_X)

Resting therefore has a hidden marginal benefit: undelivered
predation loss. The FULL marginal comparison per ton per tick is:

  Value(eat)  =  netto_eat_X  -  predation_rate_at_active
  Value(rest) =  -rest_cost   -  predation_rate_at_active * floor_X

  Value(eat) - Value(rest)
       =  netto_eat_X + rest_cost
          - (1 - floor_X) * predation_rate_at_active

where predation_rate_at_active is the per-ton, per-tick loss
suffered by fully-visible X-biomass under current predator
biomass B_Y (Holling-II saturated):

  predation_rate_at_active
       ~  B_Y * a_Y * assim_Y * E_X / (1 + a_Y * h_Y * B_X_visible)

Rest is marginally preferred (rest wins over eat) when

  (1 - floor_X) * predation_rate_at_active  >  netto_eat_X + rest_cost

For zooplankton in pzpg248nrnac (floor=0.2, pelagic_ratio~=0.26,
Type II near saturation on zoo), the RHS = 101.4 + 2.5 = 103.9
per ton per tick; the LHS reaches ~100-200 at equilibrium B_pel,
so rest%~=72% is the ecologically optimal predator-avoidance
strategy, not a YAML artefact. The pure 24.A gates falsely
flagged this as suspicious - which is exactly the pitfall this
subsection exists to prevent.

24.C USAGE GUIDELINES

  * Run 24.A on any YAML change to feeding_cost / movement_cost /
    resting_cost / max_intake_rate / assimilation / energy_content.
    Reject changes that break the hard gate for any DM.
  * If a soft-gate breach is observed in a training run, FIRST
    check whether 24.B could explain it (prey DM with floor < 1
    under non-trivial predator biomass). Only if 24.B does not
    explain the rest-heaviness should the YAML costs be revisited.
  * 24.A alone is sufficient for top predators (no predator ->
    predation_rate_at_active = 0 -> 24.B collapses to 24.A) and
    for DMs with visibility_floor >= 1.0 (no refuge, 24.B collapses
    to 24.A). Currently that covers gadoids, porpoises, seals and
    seabirds.
  * The predation-avoidance term is NOT wired into a runnable
    check; it is intentionally left as a manual back-of-envelope
    step because B_Y, B_X_visible and pi_rest_X are runtime
    quantities. If a future regression test is desired, snapshot
    them from a converged rollout and evaluate the inequality
    offline.

================================================================
25. RUNNING TRAINING/INFERENCE NON-INTERACTIVELY (gotcha)
================================================================
train.py has one remaining interactive prompt that blocks in non-tty
environments: "Continue with these parameters? [Y/n]:" - defaults to
yes on Enter. Unattended workaround:

    yes "" | python -u train.py --profile info --project mareld2.yaml \
                                --run-name <name>

`python -u` is REQUIRED - otherwise stdout is fully buffered when
stdout is a pipe.

Explicit CLI flags > profile > parser default. Inference has no
interactive prompts.

================================================================
26. SPAWN STRATEGIES PACKAGE (lib/spawn/)
================================================================
New subsystem (commit 2ef39f4) replacing the legacy uniform spread
for any FG that ships a `spawn:` YAML block.

Public API (lib/spawn/__init__.py):
  StrategySpec(mode, params, refs, seed)
  make_weights(spec, shape, context=None, project_seed=None) -> (H,W)
  distribute_with_floor(weights, total, min_per_cell,
                        allowed_mask=None) -> (H,W) biomass map

StrategySpec fields:
  mode    : "uniform" | "perlin" | "colony" | "env_driven"
  params  : mode-specific scalar dict (see schema below)
  refs    : list of {name, weight, transform} (env_driven only)
  seed    : optional int; when None, falls back to project_seed
            (which, in train.py's per-generation snapshot mode, is the
            adler32 of (gen_spawn_seed, fg_id)).

Strategies (lib/spawn/strategies.py):
  uniform     : equal weight per cell -> equivalent to legacy Dirichlet.
                No params.
  perlin      : fractal-sum Perlin noise on the cell grid.
                Params: Scale (cells/wavelength), Octaves (1-6),
                Persistence (0-1, amplitude falloff), Lacunarity (~2.0,
                frequency mult), Threshold (clamp-to-zero before
                normalisation). Pure numpy vectorised gradient noise.
  colony      : N Gaussian blobs of width sigma_cells around N random
                anchor cells.
                Params: N_colonies (int), Sigma_cells (float),
                Anchor ("free"|"coast"|"open_water"; reserved - anchor
                cells currently chosen uniformly until depth/coast map
                is wired in).
  env_driven  : weighted sum of existing env layers from `refs:`, plus
                optional Perlin overlay.
                Params: Floor (per-cell minimum before normalisation),
                Noise_amp (overlay amplitude), Noise_scale (overlay
                wavelength). Refs are looked up from
                context['env_layers'] by name; missing refs are filtered
                out at config_loader level.

Allocator (lib/spawn/allocator.py):
  distribute_with_floor implements:
    1. Ignore cells with allowed_mask==0.
    2. Pick n_active = clamp(int(total/min_per_cell), 1, n_eligible)
       cells by weight (deterministic argpartition + RNG tiebreak).
    3. Allocate floor=min_per_cell to each.
    4. Distribute the remainder proportionally to weights[active].
    5. Edge cases handled: total<floor (single cell), zero weights
       (uniform over allowed), allowed_mask all-zero (returns zeros),
       min_per_cell=0 (no floor; pure weighted distribution).
  Invariants verified by tests/test_allocator.py:
    - sum(map) == total (within fp eps)
    - all active cells >= min_per_cell
    - shape == requested shape, dtype float32
    - deterministic under same RNG seed

Integration (lib/config/config_loader.py):
  _build_spawn_spec(spawn_cfg, default_seed) parses YAML -> StrategySpec
  or returns None (legacy path).
  _derive_fg_spawn_seed(base, fg_id) -> adler32(f"{base}::{fg_id}")
  guarantees per-FG diversity under a shared generation-wide seed.
  Both setup_full_mareld_mvp and load_project_config call the same
  helper and accept `spawn_seed`.

Tests:
  tests/test_allocator.py            17 tests
  tests/test_spawn_integration.py     5 tests
  tests/test_uniform_bias_init.py    16 tests
  Total: 38, all green.

================================================================
27. PER-GENERATION SPAWN SNAPSHOT (train.py)
================================================================
Goal: every delta/worker within ONE generation sees the same biomass
maps per FG (same total AND same spatial layout), analogous to the
impact-map snapshot. Across generations the layout still varies.

Mechanism:
  _EnvBuilder grew a `spawn_seed` attribute. _make_env_builder accepts
  spawn_seed and passes it into both load_project_config and
  setup_full_mareld_mvp.
  _install_generation_maps(gen_idx) now draws two INDEPENDENT seeds:
      gen_seed       = randint(1, 2**31-1)   # impact maps
      gen_spawn_seed = randint(1, 2**31-1)   # spawn snapshot
  The new builder is created with both, then the worker pool is
  rebuilt so spawn-workers receive the updated env_builder.

In load_project_config / setup_full_mareld_mvp:
  spawn_rng_base = spawn_seed if spawn_seed is not None else seed
  fg_spawn_seed  = _derive_fg_spawn_seed(spawn_rng_base, fg_id)
  spawn_spec     = _build_spawn_spec(cfg, default_seed=fg_spawn_seed)
  spawn_rng      = np.random.default_rng(fg_spawn_seed) if spawn_seed
                   else rng
  initial_b      = _spawn_biomass_distribution(grid, total_b,
                       min_per_cell, allowed_mask=None, rng=spawn_rng,
                       spawn_spec=spawn_spec,
                       project_seed=fg_spawn_seed)
When spawn_seed is set, total_b for the FG is also sampled from a
deterministic per-FG RNG so the ENTIRE biomass map (sum + shape) is
identical across rollouts in the generation. Without spawn_seed,
legacy per-rollout behaviour is preserved bit-for-bit.

Verified empirically (sanity script):
  (1) same spawn_seed + different rollout seed -> identical biomass maps.
  (2) different spawn_seed -> maps differ.
  (3) no spawn_seed -> legacy behaviour (maps vary per rollout).
  (4) within one spawn_seed: 28 distinct per-FG layouts across the 8
      FGs of mareld2 (no two FGs share an identical pattern).

Inference mode unchanged: total_b is already deterministic from
inference_initial_biomass, so spawn_seed only affects the spatial
layout via _build_spawn_spec there.

================================================================
28. MULTI-WORLD AVERAGING PER DELTA (ARS variance reduction)
================================================================
Committed in a1484cd. Implements multi-world averaging per delta to
reduce gradient-estimate variance from the stochastic world
distribution (impact realisation + spawn layout). With M=1 the legacy
single-world path is byte-identical to pre-a1484cd behaviour.

Concept:
  Each ARS iteration draws a WorldList of length M:
      WorldList = [(impact_seed_1, spawn_seed_1), ..., (imp_M, sp_M)]
  For every delta i and every sign in {+, -}, the trainer evaluates
  M independent rollouts (one per world) and averages reward + act
  diagnostics:
      r_pos_i = mean_m R(theta + sigma*delta_i, world_m)
      r_neg_i = mean_m R(theta - sigma*delta_i, world_m)
  CRN coupling: +delta and -delta for the SAME (i, m) pair share the
  SAME world AND the SAME pair_seed[i]. This makes Var(r_pos - r_neg)
  drop faster than 1/M (world-wise anti-correlation).
  Top-b ranking happens on the M-averaged r_pos/r_neg vectors, so it
  is much less brittle than under the world noise of M=1.

CLI (Section 5):
  --rollouts_per_delta M       Static M. Profiles force 3; default 1.
  --worlds_refresh POLICY      "iteration" (default; resample each
                               ARS iter) or "generation" (lock for
                               whole gen).
  --rollouts_per_delta_schedule "M0@G0,M1@G1,..."
                               e.g. "1@0,3@10,5@50". Overrides
                               --rollouts_per_delta.

Seed bookkeeping (train.py):
  _world_rng = np.random.default_rng()  (independent of np global RNG)
  _sample_world_list(M) -> [(impact_seed, spawn_seed), ...] drawn
      from _world_rng (so future --seed plumbing is trivial).
  _resolve_M(gen, args) -> int (schedule lookup or static).
  _parse_M_schedule(spec) -> sorted [(gen_threshold, M), ...].
  _refresh_world_list_if_needed(gen, iter) -> bool
      Returns True iff the WorldList actually (re)sampled. Publishes
      to _current_world_list AND trainer.world_list. Refresh rules:
        * iter == 0 within a generation -> always (re)sample.
        * worlds_refresh == "iteration" -> resample every iter.
        * worlds_refresh == "generation" -> no resample for iter>0.

Trainer integration (lib/runners/trainer.py):
  self.world_list = []                    (populated by train.py)
  _resolve_world_builders(): returns [self.env_builder] when M<=1
      (legacy tuple-task path), otherwise [env_builder.with_world(imp,
      sp) for imp, sp in world_list] -> M sibling builders.
  train_step / train_step_coevolution: M-loop wraps the existing
      (delta_i, sign) loop. Tasks are dict-format when M>1 carrying
      'env_builder' override per task; tuple-format when M==1 for
      byte-identical legacy worker path.
  Averaging helpers (null-tolerant): _avg_act_diags, _avg_act_dicts,
      _avg_fit_dicts. Reward and act_diag are averaged over M; fitness
      shape unchanged so the rest of the ARS update is intact.

Worker (lib/runners/parallel_worker.py):
  _evaluate_task / _evaluate_coevo_task accept BOTH tuple and dict
  task formats. Dict format may carry an 'env_builder' override per
  task; falls back to module-global _ENV_BUILDER (legacy) otherwise.

_EnvBuilder.with_world(impact_seed, spawn_seed):
  Returns a fresh sibling builder with re-sampled impact maps and a
  locked spawn_seed; all other state (grid, project, impact recipe)
  is inherited. Picklable for spawn-context workers.

Profile defaults: sanity / info / deep all set rollouts_per_delta=3.
Override via explicit --rollouts_per_delta on the CLI.

Pre-prompt header (train.py): prints a "Rollouts/Delta" line showing
either the static M (with user/default/profile marking) or the
schedule string, plus the worlds_refresh policy.

Cost: total rollouts per iteration = 2 * n_deltas * M. The worker
pool's _auto_workers cap at 2*n_deltas means M>1 utilises more
parallelism than M=1, so wall-time scales sub-linearly with M up to
the CPU core count.

Open follow-ups (not implemented yet):
  * Adaptive M based on gradient-norm CV or top-b rank instability
    (sketched in Section 28 of the original design discussion).
  * A/B variance-reduction test (M=1 vs M=5) on a deterministic seed
    sequence to quantify the empirical win.
  * --seed plumbing into _world_rng for fully reproducible WorldLists.

================================================================
29. PROBE BIOMASS LOGGING & DIAGNOSTICS UPDATES
================================================================
Late-session additions on top of Sections 27/28. Purely diagnostic;
no change to ARS math or env semantics.

29.1 Per-iteration probe rollout (train.py)
  * Helper _ProbeEnvBuilder builds a deterministic "reference world"
    using load_project_config(..., mode='inference') so spawn totals
    come from inference_initial_biomass (scaled to runtime grid via
    biomass_scale, see Section 7b) and impact maps are loaded from
    inference.impact_maps (.npz files) instead of being sampled.
  * Fixed PROBE_SEED = 20260530; same env state every iteration so
    bh/b0 reflects policy change only (CRN-style isolation from
    training's WorldList variance).
  * Helper _probe_biomass(trainer, probe_builder, n_ticks, gen, it,
    jsonl_path) installs current theta + frozen obs-norm stats from
    trainer.obs_stats, runs n_ticks ticks, and emits:
      - stdout (compact percent format):
          [probe gen=N] fid: NN% | fid: NN% | ...
      - JSONL append at results/<run-name>/biomass.jsonl with fields
        {gen, iter, n_ticks, b0, bh, ratio, log10_ratio}.
  * Called after each ARS iteration in both round-robin and co-evo
    paths.

29.2 Soft action-mass diagnostics (ecosystem.py)
  * Earlier mv/rs/et accounting used argmax (winner-takes-all per
    cell), which can be misleading when the policy spreads mass
    across categories. The predation/movement modules use the SOFT
    pi_move / pi_rest / pi_eat distributions directly, not argmax.
  * _action_move/rest/eat_frac now accumulate per-cell category mass:
      move = sum_{0..3} probs (4 move dirs)
      rest = probs[4]
      eat  = sum_{5..5+N_all-1} probs (all prey eat-nodes)
    averaged over active cells (mask = biomass>0) per tick.
  * Denominator is _action_active_ticks[i] (per-DM, Section "alt-1"
    fix), so mv+rs+et == 1 exactly per DM and H_act is conditional
    entropy given the FG is alive somewhere.
  * Consumer format strings in trainer.py / parallel_worker.py are
    unchanged - field names kept.

29.3 present_frac in coevo status line (trainer.py)
  * _avg_act_dicts propagates 'present_frac' across the M-world
    average when present in all per-world act_diag entries.
  * [coevo fid] line now prints "present=NN%" after the H_act block
    when data is available, exposing FG absence/extinction during
    rollouts in the live log. Format example:
      [coevo zooplankton] mv/rs/et=0.00/0.88/0.12
        H_act=0.27/2.565 (11%) present=98%
        r_mean=+1.4e+00 r_std=... rel_std=... sigma_f=...
  * Order of fields in both [diag ...] (round-robin) and [coevo ...]
    is now: act_str (mv/rs/et then H_act, optionally present) first,
    then reward stats.

29.4 Probe stdout format
  * Changed from log10(bh/b0) to human-readable percent per FG with
    " | " separators. JSONL retains both ratio and log10_ratio for
    downstream analysis (symmetric scales, geometric means).

29.5 growth_rate (MG_X) wiring for decision makers
  * Per Method.pdf p.18/p.29, MG_X is required for all energy-modeled
    groups (= all DMs), used in B(t+1) = clip(B*(1 + MG_X*q_X), 0, inf).
  * fg_library.yaml previously omitted growth_rate for DMs, making
    params.get('growth_rate', 0.0) the silent default and disabling
    the starvation/growth branch entirely. Now populated:
      zooplankton 0.05, pelagic_fish 0.01, gadoids 0.005,
      porpoises/seals/seabirds 0.002 (initial values; user may have
      since multiplied top-predator values by 5x to bring the
      starvation channel onto the rollout time scale).
  * fgconfig/fgconfig.py: DM editor main_props now exposes
      ("Max Growth (MG_X, fraction/tick)", "growth_rate", "entry")
    so the field is editable in the GUI for DMs (it was already
    present for NDMs).

29.6 Open structural items (still applicable)
  * benthic_community grows unbounded (no DM predates it).
  * min_split_biomass=50 kg kept for seals/porpoises; cluster-spawn
    + sub-threshold one-hot collapse (Section 20) handles it during
    training, but per-cell density is sensitive on small grids.
  * Eat fraction is logged as the sum over all prey eat-nodes; split
    per prey is available in probs but not currently surfaced.

================================================================
30. ENV_DRIVEN REFS (zoo follows phyto in spawn)
================================================================
Feature implemented earlier this arc: any FG can now spawn following
another FG's biomass layout via `spawn.refs`. Works in BOTH training
and inference (load_project_config is mode-agnostic for the refs
pipeline).

YAML format (per-FG override in project OR library default):
  spawn:
    mode: env_driven
    floor: 0.0
    noise_amp: 0.0
    noise_scale: 12.0
    refs:
      - {name: phytoplankton, weight: 1.0, transform: linear}
      # transform: linear | exp | invert | gauss_smooth

Pipeline (lib/config/config_loader.py):
  * Topological sort over FG-spawn DAG built from env_driven refs.
    Cycles degrade gracefully to declared order (warning only).
  * env_fields accumulator passed via env_context={'env_fields': dict}
    to _spawn_biomass_distribution. After each FG is spawned its
    initial_b is published as env_fields[fg_id] so downstream env_driven
    FGs see it.
  * Verified empirically: zoo with refs=[{phytoplankton, 1.0}] gets
    corr(zoo_layout, phyto_layout) = 1.000 in both train and inference.

GUI (fgconfig/fgconfig.py):
  * env_driven mode shows a refs-editor (FG-name dropdown + signed
    weight + transform dropdown + Add/Remove buttons).
  * _collect_spawn_dict serialises `refs:` to YAML; _populate_spawn_editor
    reads back. Live preview uses synthetic Perlin-per-ref so the user
    sees topology before saving.

================================================================
31. ENERGY_GAIN INVARIANT (auto-sync in FG editor)
================================================================
Invariant: interaction.energy_gain MUST equal prey.energy_content. The
field is a redundant copy and was the cause of historical drift in
fg_library.yaml (6/8 relations had energy_gain != prey.energy_content
before the fix).

State of the field:
  * NO write path in fgconfig.py creates/updates energy_gain via the
    matrix editor (that editor only exposes preys_on + max_intake_rate).
  * Only TWO reads: apply_fg_changes hard-gate (rad ~2549-2551) uses
    energy_gain if present, else falls back to prey.energy_content.
  * Runtime (config_loader, ecosystem) uses the same fallback.

Auto-sync (apply_fg_changes in fgconfig.py):
  After writing species_definitions[fg_id], iterates all
  *_preys_on_<fg_id>-entries in interaction_definitions with
  preys_on=true and rewrites energy_gain to the new energy_content.
  Reports synced relations via messagebox.

When new FG is added: no energy_gain is written by any path (matrix
editor doesn't have the column). Relations rely on the fallback
prey.energy_content. Self-healing only kicks in next time the prey
FG's energy_content is changed via FG editor - fine because new
relations were never in drift to begin with.

Open: a hypothetical "Fix B" (strip energy_gain entirely from YAML +
all reads, rely solely on prey.energy_content) was discussed but not
implemented. Current state is invariant-safe via auto-sync.

================================================================
32. FG EDITOR SILENT-OVERRIDE FIXES
================================================================
Three silent data-loss paths in apply_fg_changes / _collect_spawn_dict
were closed:

Fix 1 (spawn params parse failure):
  _collect_spawn_dict accepts optional `errors=[]`. When set, int/float
  parse failures append to errors instead of dropping the param via
  `continue`. apply_fg_changes calls with errors=[] and aborts via
  messagebox.showerror if non-empty.

Fix 2 (env_driven ref weight):
  Same `errors` mechanism - invalid weight no longer silently coerces
  to 1.0; aborts apply with explicit error message.

Fix 3 (FG property parse, e.g. "1,5" Swedish comma):
  apply_fg_changes property loop: parse failure now shows
  messagebox.showerror and refuses to save. Empty string still maps
  to 0.0 (legacy contract for blanked fields).

Live preview path uses lenient mode (no errors=) so mid-edit values
like "0." don't spam warnings.

Open (lower priority, not fixed):
  * Silent clamps in apply_fg_changes (movement_speed > 1 -> 1, etc.)
  * Library vs project file sync (Apply writes library only; project
    needs manual "Save Project").
  * Spawn mode-switch defaults overwrite previous values.
  * Schema defaults written to YAML on Apply even if user changed
    nothing.
  * Initial biomass range live validation can block keystrokes.

33. NDM CLEANUP: resting_metabolism/max_energy_reserve REMOVED
================================================================
Discovery: `_apply_growth` non-DM branch uses ONLY growth_rate and
max_carrying_capacity (logistic). It NEVER reads resting_metabolism,
max_energy_reserve, feeding_cost, etc. Cleaned up dead data from YAML:

  fgconfig/fg_library.yaml:
    phytoplankton: removed resting_metabolism: 0.1 + max_energy_reserve: 0.0
    benthic_community: removed resting_metabolism: 0.2 + max_energy_reserve: 0.0

GUI ndm_props deliberately does NOT expose resting_metabolism - design
choice consistent with runtime semantics.
